# Image credits (dev catalog)

The photos in `dev-catalog/images/` are used **only** by the fake Taobao provider and the `dev`/`test` profiles
(served at `/api/v1/dev/images/**` by `DevCatalogImages`). They are not in the jar or the Docker image.

All photos are from [Unsplash](https://unsplash.com) under the [Unsplash License](https://unsplash.com/license):
free to use, commercially or not, without permission; attribution is not required but is given here.
Unsplash+ photos are not used. Files are resized and cropped (products 800x800, category covers 800x500) and saved
as WebP.
